address on a configured interface and sending Router Advertisements for peer discovery. (cilium/cilium#47394, @ecbaldwin)\r\n* Enable BPF masquerading by default (cilium/cilium#49411, @pchaigno)\r\n* gateway-api: Add frontend client certificate validation (mTLS) support. (cilium/cilium#48587, @arybolovlev)\r\n* gateway-api: add initial support for listener TLS version options. (cilium/cilium#49063, @arybolovlev)\r\n* network-driver: support Multi Pool Resource IPAM in the agent (cilium/cilium#49222, @andreaterzolo)\r\n\r\n**Minor Changes:**\r\n* [network-driver] Add multi-pool Resource IPAM support in the operator (cilium/cilium#48764, @pippolo84)\r\n* Add Gateway API Inference Extension support (cilium/cilium#48969, @xtineskim)\r\n* Add ingressController.service.insecurePortEnabled and ingressController.service.securePortEnabled to control whether the shared Cilium Ingress Service exposes port 80 and port 443. (cilium/cilium#48870, @bitflicker64)\r\n* Add runtime reconciliation and garbage collection for endpoint routing rules in ENI, Azure, and AlibabaCloud IPv4 IPAM modes. (cilium/cilium#48383, @pippolo84)\r\n* alibabacloud: support IMDSv2 (token-based) instance metadata access (cilium/cilium#49182, @4n4nd)\r\n* alibabacloud: support RRSA (RAM Roles for Service Accounts) credentials in the IPAM allocator, alongside static AccessKeys and ECS instance RAM roles (cilium/cilium#49093, @4n4nd)\r\n* cilium-cli: fall back to WebSocket when a proxy rejects the SPDY upgrade (cilium/cilium#49229, @epb-644)\r\n* datapath: Prioritize preferred IPv6 addresses over deprecated ones during node-address selection. (cilium/cilium#49451, @kyyril)\r\n* datapath: turn `DEBUG_TAGGED` into runtime config (cilium/cilium#49393, @ekoops)\r\n* Deprecate XDP prefilter (cilium/cilium#49589, @joestringer)\r\n* docs: document Cilium installation on ACK BYOCNI (cilium/cilium#49258, @l1b0k)\r\n* Don't select IPv4/IPv6 addresses flagged deprecated when determining the node address (cilium/cilium#47878, @arcusbuilds)\r\n* egressgw: Support egress IPs that the gateway node owns through a local route instead of an interface address, such as the IPs of GCE forwarding rules. (cilium/cilium#49400, @sudeephb)\r\n* feat/ces: cilium-operator now patches CES before deleting it whenever CES has at least `--ces-empty-before-delete-threshold` number of endpoints. (cilium/cilium#49215, @kamilWyszynski1)\r\n* gateway-api: BackendTLSPolicy is now supported for GRPCRoutes. (cilium/cilium#49130, @adilalperenciftci)\r\n* gateway-api: Fix Gateway API controllers failing to start in clusters with many namespaces when a Gateway listener uses an `allowedRoutes` namespace selector (cilium/cilium#49238, @Nevon)\r\n* helm: Add attachMetadata option to ServiceMonitors (cilium/cilium#49259, @kirill-iakovlev)\r\n* helm: Add more extension hooks for Hubble Relay (cilium/cilium#49097, @devodev)\r\n* Hubble flows now include Kubernetes workload information for endpoints on remote nodes. (cilium/cilium#48563, @devodev)\r\n* hubble: Fix typo in --port-forward flag description (cilium/cilium#49126, @neronkang)\r\n* ipam: Remove `--bypass-ip-availability-upon-restore` flag (cilium/cilium#49135, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* k8s: Use omitzero for struct fields of CiliumNode and CiliumEndpoint (cilium/cilium#49220, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* Make internal label handling more memory-efficient (cilium/cilium#49692, @1stDimension)\r\n* metrics: Added cilium_agent_memory_bytes, an estimate of agent memory usage broken down by component, so that BPF memlock is accounted for alongside the Go heap. (cilium/cilium#48227, @mvisonneau)\r\n* Migrate AlibabaCloud IPAM to the Multi-pool allocator (cilium/cilium#49303, @TonyNee)\r\n* Move cloud-IPAM endpoint route and policy-rule management from CNI to the Cilium agent. (cilium/cilium#48384, @pippolo84)\r\n* nat: Don't masquerade node InternalIP addresses (cilium/cilium#49344, @pchaigno)\r\n* network driver: add RX queue manager (cilium/cilium#49520, @aarcamp)\r\n* network driver: allow setting interface sysctl for allocated devices (cilium/cilium#48711, @bersoare)\r\n* network driver: prepare NICs for RX queue leasing (cilium/cilium#49651, @aarcamp)\r\n* network driver: support DRA consumable capacity (cilium/cilium#48863, @aarcamp)\r\n* operator/ipam: A node's attached IPs change in cloud IPAM modes now reconciles only that node instead of every node in the cluster. This decreases the volume of operator CiliumNode writes and pool maintenance passes on large clusters. (cilium/cilium#49687, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* pkg/aws: Secondary ENIs get an IPv6 configured to their netlink devices to make sure nodeports work when using kube-proxy. (cilium/cilium#48652, @41ks)\r\n* proxy: Bump envoy version to 1.40.x (cilium/cilium#49491, @sayboras)\r\n* Restore L2 announcements for Gateway API and Ingress LoadBalancer Services that use externalTrafficPolicy: Local. (cilium/cilium#49003, @lavish-gambhir)\r\n* The deprecated option `--tofqdns-pre-cache` has been removed. (cilium/cilium#49124, @HadrienPatte)\r\n\r\n**Bugfixes:**\r\n* Allow wildcard CIDR selectors to match nodes when --policy-cidr-match-mode=nodes is configured (cilium/cilium#47828, @TheBeeZee)\r\n* azure: Fixed an issue where an interface with an empty MAC field would be reported back the the cilium agent causing it to crash on startup when trying to configure the routes. (cilium/cilium#48648, @41ks)\r\n* bpf: nat: fix outer ICMP checksum for ICMP/SCTP NAT error packets (cilium/cilium#48819, @fds7280)\r\n* cilium-dbg: Print port numbers correctly for map cilium_lb*_reverse_sk (cilium/cilium#47134, @jayl1e)\r\n* CiliumEnvoyConfig now only redirects Services in its own namespace, as documented; service references in CiliumClusterwideEnvoyConfig without a namespace default to \"default\" again. (cilium/cilium#49076, @maxskokov)\r\n* datapath/config: use buffered writer for `#include \"lib/utils.h\"` (cilium/cilium#49342, @ekoops)\r\n* datapath: Fix NAT46x64 gateway prefix conversion panic (cilium/cilium#49177, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* endpoint: use final regeneration error to resolve rollback state (cilium/cilium#49471, @jrajahalme)\r\n* envoy: drain proxy completions on cancellation and early errors (cilium/cilium#49489, @jrajahalme)\r\n* Extend L7 redirect ID defintion with L7 parser type, so that http and tls rules on same endpoint/port use separate proxy listeners. (cilium/cilium#49549, @nezdolik)\r\n* feat/ces: omitzero tag for CoreCiliumEndpoint (cilium/cilium#49186, @kamilWyszynski1)\r\n* Fix a leak of load balancer BPF map entries for NodePort services that have different external and internal traffic policies. (cilium/cilium#49373, @vt0r)\r\n* Fix a race in AWS, Alibaba Cloud, and Azure IPAM where partial resyncs could overwrite newer cache updates, making released IPs appear available again. (cilium/cilium#49620, @pippolo84)\r\n* Fix accidental reintroduction of the Envoy policy for a removed endpoint, which would then cause persistent Envoy NACK upon endpoint IP reuse. (cilium/cilium#49652, @jrajahalme)\r\n* Fix agent bootstrap to order endpoint regeneration after DNS proxy initialization to prevent proxy port race. (cilium/cilium#49540, @jrajahalme)\r\n* Fix an issue where terminating backends were evicted from BPF maps when trafficDistribution was enabled. (cilium/cilium#48349, @taesung320)\r\n* Fix connections to a pod timing out when the client reuses the source port of a closed NodePort or HostPort connection to that pod. (cilium/cilium#47641, @aanm)\r\n* Fix egress policy rules that combine `toServices` with `toPorts` allowing traffic to any destination on those ports when the referenced Service doesn't exist or is selector-less with no backends. (cilium/cilium#49430, @christarazi)\r\n* Fix etcd connectivity diagnostics to honor the configured timeout when reading the heartbeat key on high-latency connections. (cilium/cilium#49562, @grzesrap)\r\n* Fix HTTP URL query not being redacted in Hubble flows when the Envoy access log URL fails to parse. (cilium/cilium#49427, @sage-mode-hunter)\r\n* Fix Hubble dynamic metrics retaining deleted-pod series by applying endpoint-deletion cleanup to the active dynamic metric handlers. (cilium/cilium#49712, @alexcastilio)\r\n* Fix invalid L4 checksum on IPv6 ClusterIP reverse NAT for CHECKSUM_PARTIAL packets on kernels < 6.12, affecting the case when packets are bridged/tunneled from the client pod to a remote host (cilium/cilium#49204, @liuyuan10)\r\n* Fix ip6tables chain rename errors being silently ignored during iptables rule reconciliation. (cilium/cilium#49024, @maxskokov)\r\n* Fix multi-pool IPAM spreading pod IPs across multiple CIDRs instead of filling the oldest CIDRs first. This kept unused IPs from being released, so nodes held more CIDRs than they needed. (cilium/cilium#49418, @41ks)\r\n* Fix nodes sending out ARP probes for their own IPs when L2 neighbor discovery is enabled. (cilium/cilium#49404, @rtapadar)\r\n* Fix regression in Cilium 1.20.3 where the Gateway API implementation would incorrectly handle BackendTLSPolicy configurations, leading to high Cilium CPU usage and inability to forward traffic. (cilium/cilium#49525, @youngnick)\r\n* Fix restored endpoints getting only policy rules that select all endpoints. (cilium/cilium#49091, @jaredledvina)\r\n* Fix strict egress encryption allowing unencrypted traffic when --encryption-strict-egress-cidr contains host bits. (cilium/cilium#49446, @fds7280)\r\n* Fix the bandwidth manager dropping all egress traffic of a rate-limited pod for up to tens of seconds after a single packet carried a departure time beyond the drop horizon. (cilium/cilium#49653, @IraSkyx)\r\n* Fix the operator allocating IPv4 addresses and IPv4-bearing ENIs to IPv6-only nodes in ENI IPAM mode. (cilium/cilium#49107, @41ks)\r\n* Fix the operator releasing ENI multi-pool IPs below min-allocate, causing nodes to immediately allocate the IPs again. (cilium/cilium#49125, @jaredledvina)\r\n* Fix two services ending up with the same service ID after an agent restart, and repair nodes where this already happened. (cilium/cilium#49224, @tahsinrahman)\r\n* fix: Overlay revSNAT entry missed extended masquerade port range fallback (cilium/cilium#48229, @weizhoublue)\r\n* Fixed a goroutine leak that could cause Cilium agents to accumulate thousands of goroutines under sustained CiliumNode churn, increasing CPU usage and contributing to latency or packet drops. (cilium/cilium#46257, @evevseev)\r\n* Fixed an issue where DNS egress policy could deny requests after a destination’s security identity changed. DNS proxy rules now refresh alongside incremental policy updates. (cilium/cilium#49699, @tklauser)\r\n* Fixes a bug where the helm value `hubble.tls.auto.privateKey` settings are no longer ignored in Hubble cert-manager certificates (cilium/cilium#49114, @byxorna)\r\n* Fixes an issue in LBIPAM where on service upsert, if IP allocations are not changed the sharing index is not updated. (cilium/cilium#48938, @0xch4z)\r\n* Fixes an issue where CiliumCIDRGroups with zero-length cidrs (i.e. 0.0.0.0/0) inadvertently selected no traffic. (cilium/cilium#48366, @squeed)\r\n* gateway-api: fix an issue when routes order might not be consistent between reconciliations. (cilium/cilium#49504, @arybolovlev)\r\n* gateway-api: make HTTP route ordering deterministic (cilium/cilium#48951, @abduu11aahh)\r\n* gateway-api: Prevent redundant Envoy updates from equal-precedence GRPCRoute reordering (cilium/cilium#49354, @weizhoublue)\r\n* gateway-api: Support system CA certificates in BackendTLSPolicy. (cilium/cilium#48895, @shreyasrajiv327)\r\n* gwapi: fix a case where all listeners moving from valid to invalid leaves stale Envoy config installed (cilium/cilium#49310, @asauber)\r\n* helm: Add node-role.kubernetes.io/etcd to default cilium-operator tolerations (cilium/cilium#49371, @ankitaojha123)\r\n* infraendpoints: Fixed a bug where an agent, in IPAM ENI mode, fails to set up router routes and rules because the ENI interface is not UP yet. (cilium/cilium#49267, @41ks)\r\n* ipam: prevent full resyncs from overwriting cache updates (cilium/cilium#49008, @aanm)\r\n* ipsec: allow plaintext overlay traffic during migration (cilium/cilium#49245, @sjohnsonpal)\r\n* ipsec: propagate errors from local node IN route add/remove updates to node update handling (cilium/cilium#48775, @AdeshDeshmukh)\r\n* l2announcer: Fix L2 announcements of new IPs stopping until the agent restarts after many selected services are removed at once (cilium/cilium#49645, @aanm)\r\n* loadbalancer: Fixes to cleaning up stale data after BPF reconciliation had encountered an error and the service or endpoints had changed before retry was performed (cilium/cilium#48919, @joamaki)\r\n* network driver: scope advertised devices to the node by using the node name in the resourceSlice pool. (cilium/cilium#48922, @bersoare)\r\n* nodediscovery: in IPAM ENI and Azure modes the operator now treats nodes as running in multi-pool mode from the very first event. (cilium/cilium#49242, @41ks)\r\n* option: validate vtep-mask is an IPv4 address (cilium/cilium#49271, @wrzwrz123)\r\n* Reduce transient DNS SERVFAIL/REFUSED for pods that query immediately at startup by pushing/applying Standalone DNS Proxy policy snapshots more frequently (cilium/cilium#49112, @QxBytes)\r\n* Remove the unused pebble binary from the cilium image to clear its Go standard library vulnerability findings. (Backport PR cilium/cilium#48907, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48491, @ajmmm)\r\n* Fix PMTU/ICMP replies for IPIP/Geneve DSR for GRO skbs (Backport PR cilium/cilium#49189, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48489, @borkmann)\r\n* Fix rare data race in selector cache that could lead to agent panic (Backport PR cilium/cilium#49189, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#47733, @odinuge)\r\n* Fix restoration of terminating backends. (Backport PR cilium/cilium#48797, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48763, @joamaki)\r\n* Fixes a bug where Hubble flows may list the incorrect policy that allowed or denied a flow (Backport PR cilium/cilium#49189, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#49086, @tsotne95)\r\n* Fixes a bug where the helm value `hubble.tls.auto.privateKey` settings are no longer ignored in Hubble cert-manager certificates (Backport PR cilium/cilium#49189, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#49114, @byxorna)\r\n* Fixes an issue where CiliumCIDRGroups with zero-length cidrs (i.e. 0.0.0.0/0) inadvertently selected no traffic. (Backport PR cilium/cilium#49336, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48366, @squeed)\r\n* gateway-api: filter CEC controls from infrastructure metadata (Backport PR cilium/cilium#48984, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#47732, @nezdolik)\r\n* gateway-api: make HTTP route ordering deterministic (Backport PR cilium/cilium#49336, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48951, @abduu11aahh)\r\n* gateway-api: Support system CA certificates in BackendTLSPolicy. (Backport PR cilium/cilium#49336, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48895, @shreyasrajiv327)\r\n* infraendpoints: Release the health IPv4 when the IPv6 allocation fails (Backport PR cilium/cilium#48907, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48816, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* k8s/resource: fix leaked event finalizer on unobserved delete (Backport PR cilium/cilium#48907, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48785, @tklauser)\r\n* L7 conformance CI fixes (Backport PR cilium/cilium#48999, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#47517, @fristonio)\r\n* operator: Do not emit warning when deleting already deleted unmanaged pod (Backport PR cilium/cilium#48907, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48765, @gandro)\r\n* operator: fix `metrics list` default address to work on IPv6-only clusters with dual stack nodes by trying both IPv4 and IPv6 loopback addresses (Backport PR cilium/cilium#48907, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#48780, @41ks)\r\n* Set ipv6 routing rules when dual stack is enabled (Backport PR cilium/cilium#49336, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#49188, @fcrespofastly)\r\n* Sockets are no longer destroyed when a Service backend is terminated unless they are still connected to that backend. Unconnected UDP sockets, and sockets that have since disconnected or moved to another backend, used to have their local port silently changed. No user-facing change on its own. (cilium/cilium#47813, @amaanx86)\r\n* auth: remove deprecated mutual authentication (cilium/cilium#48647, @mhofstetter)\r\n* clang: Bump llvm version to v21.1.8 (cilium/cilium#48425, @sayboras)\r\n* Fix a \"No route to host\" window on node reboot where BGP advertised routes before BPF programs were attached to native devices. (cilium/cilium#46948, @tkna)\r\n* Fix conntrack garbage collection deleting CT entries that the datapath reopened between the GC batch read and the delete, which could cascade into NAT mapping deletion and SNAT source-port reallocation for live connections. (cilium/cilium#46750, @gyutaeb)\r\n* Hubble observer server hardening (cilium/cilium#46290, @fristonio)\r\n* Hubble Relay startup can now be composed by downstream binaries with additional server options. (cilium/cilium#48461, @devodev)\r\n* IPsec subnet encryption under ENI, Azure and AlibabaCloud now derives its pod subnets from the VPC CIDRs directly, fixing cases where they were empty (no encryption policies installed) depending on agent startup ordering. AlibabaCloud gains subnet encryption, and the ip-masq-agent non-masquerade CIDRs are no longer treated as pod subnets. (cilium/cilium#48493, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* LB IPAM: whitespace around the namespaces in the lbipam.cilium.io/sharing-cross-namespace annotation no longer prevents services from sharing an IP. (cilium/cilium#48868, @lexfrei)\r\n* Migrate Azure IPAM to the Multi-pool allocator (cilium/cilium#48449, @jaredledvina)\r\n* network driver: operator managed node configs (cilium/cilium#47597, @bersoare)\r\n* network driver: track DRA allocations separately from device inventory (cilium/cilium#48755, @aarcamp)\r\n* Setting \"policyCidrMatchMode=nodes\" no longer causes unnecessary node identities to be allocated, saving space in the IPCache and policy maps. (cilium/cilium#48292, @squeed)\r\n* The agent option `--fixed-identity-mapping` is deprecated. It will be removed in a future release. (cilium/cilium#48946, @squeed)\r\n* Track peer AllowedIPs as netip.Prefix (cilium/cilium#47815, @Wise-Wizard)\r\n\r\n**Bugfixes:**\r\n* bpf: Fix ICMPv6 checksum handling after NAT of embedded SCTP packets. of Changes\r\n------------------\r\n\r\n**Major Changes:**\r\n* Envoy resources (e.g., Listeners, Network Policies, etc.) can now be synchronized with Envoy incrementally, reducing CPU load and policy update latency. This is enabled by the new agent option value `--envoy-xds-mode=delta-split`. Corresponding Helm value is `envoy.xdsMode=delta-split`. (cilium/cilium#45288, @jrajahalme)\r\n* network driver: add agent side baseline driver and dummy device manager (cilium/cilium#47254, @bersoare)\r\n\r\n**Minor Changes:**\r\n* build: Update iptables from 1.8.8 to 1.8.11 (cilium/cilium#47623, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* clustermesh: no longer restart pods when changing remote clustermesh-apiserver IPs statically defined (without a DNS name) (cilium/cilium#47190, @MrFreezeex)\r\n* CT map adaptive GC interval calculation improvements (cilium/cilium#46796, @fristonio)\r\n* Deprecate WireGuard Node Encryption (cilium/cilium#47219, @pchaigno)\r\n* envoy: demote stale ADS endpoint warning (cilium/cilium#47148, @nezdolik)\r\n* Fix external traffic drops/inconsistencies on secondary ENIs with masquerade enabled (cilium/cilium#47008, @monta-riahi)\r\n* helm: Add extraInitContainers and disableDefaultVolumes to support external certificate management for Hubble and ClusterMesh components. (cilium/cilium#46688, @alaashehab-m)\r\n* helm: add GatewayAPI HTTPRoute support for hubble-ui (cilium/cilium#46792, @SchulteMK)\r\n* helm: add value to configure clustermesh apiserver service port (cilium/cilium#47375, @crealte)\r\n* network driver: add Cilium Network Driver CRDs, helm values and CRD chart entries (cilium/cilium#46238, @bersoare)\r\n* network driver: add cluster roles and mounts for the network driver feature (cilium/cilium#46995, @bersoare)\r\n* Removed the deprecated `cidr` field from `CiliumNode.Status.Azure` interfaces and the deprecated `subnet` field from their addresses; use `interfaces[].subnet.{id,cidr}` instead. (cilium/cilium#47461, @jaredledvina)\r\n* Speed up recovery time for disrupted TCP connections that access a DSR-enabled Service. (cilium/cilium#47529, @julianwiedmann)\r\n\r\n**Bugfixes:**\r\n* azure: Stop issuing redundant CiliumNode status updates on every IPAM sync when the node's Azure interfaces are unchanged. (cilium/cilium#47449, @jaredledvina)\r\n* bpf: hostfw: tolerate unknown CT protocols and rely on policies (cilium/cilium#47343, @smagnani96)\r\n* datapath/linux/config: fix IPV4_DIRECT_ROUTING selection on lo device (cilium/cilium#46861, @fdomain)\r\n* endpoint/watchdog: Avoid warning about endpoints being deleted (cilium/cilium#47625, @christarazi)\r\n* envoy: restore http-idle-timeout as the route idle timeout source (cilium/cilium#47583, @aanm)\r\n* Fix a spurious \"unable to find ifindex for interface MAC\" agent warning on EKS ENI IPAM by waiting for the ENI netlink interface before configuring ingress routes and rules. (cilium/cilium#47295, @aanm)\r\n* Fix abnormal ip allocation caused by hostnetwork pod (cilium/cilium#47552, @haozhangami)\r\n* Fix unintended RevDNAT for client-to-pod TCP connections, when an identical connection was previously established through a DSR Service. (cilium/cilium#47593, @julianwiedmann)\r\n* Fixed an issue where an HTTPRoute referencing a Gateway with mixed listener protocols (e.g. HTTP and TCP) was incorrectly rejected with `NotAllowedByListeners` when the TCP listener had an explicit `AllowedRoutes.Kinds` restriction. (cilium/cilium#46842, @pidreher)\r\n* gateway-api: preserve duplicate HTTPRoute rule precedence (cilium/cilium#46696, @thorn3r)\r\n* gateway-api: prevent conflicted listeners from reaching ingestion (cilium/cilium#47457, @asauber)\r\n* gateway-api: sync ListenerSet TLS secrets on ListenerSet events (cilium/cilium#47627, @mhofstetter)\r\n* hubble: fix four config knobs that never reach their sink (cilium/cilium#47637, @aanm)\r\n* hubble: Fixed false-positive increments of the `hubble_ring_buffer` lost events metric. **Release Announcement** 🎉: We are excited to announce the [Cilium 1.20.0](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/releases/tag/v1.20.0) release!\r\n\r\nMore than **2,660 new commits** have landed in this release, supported by a community of over **1,100 contributors** and more than **24,800 GitHub stars**! 🤩\r\n\r\n⚠️ You may need to take action during the upgrade if you use legacy Mutual Authentication, Envoy Go extensions or Kafka-aware policies, the `cilium.io/v2alpha1` `CiliumNodeConfig` API, the libnetwork integration, or a custom CNI configuration. See the [Upgrade Guide](https://docs.cilium.io/en/v1.20/operations/upgrade/#upgrade-notes) for details.\r\n\r\nThe full changelog can be found [here](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/blob/v1.20/CHANGELOG.md).\r\n\r\nHere are some of the highlights:\r\n\r\n* ⛩️ **Gateway API**\r\n    * 🚀 **Gateway API v1.6.1**: Cilium moves from Gateway API v1.4 to v1.6.1, bringing support for capabilities that graduated across both upstream releases. ([cilium/cilium#45251](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45251), @youngnick; [cilium/cilium#46827](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46827), [cilium/cilium#47274](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/47274), @arybolovlev)\r\n    * 👥 **Delegate Gateway Listeners**: ListenerSets let application teams attach and manage their own listeners while the platform team retains ownership of the shared Gateway. ([cilium/cilium#46303](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46303), [cilium/cilium#46785](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46785), @asauber)\r\n    * 🔏 **Encrypt Traffic to Backends**: Gateway API `BackendTLSPolicy` support lets operators configure TLS and backend certificate validation for traffic between the gateway and application services. ([cilium/cilium#43045](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43045), @youngnick)\r\n    * 🔀 **TCPRoute and UDPRoute**: Databases, DNS servers, game servers and other non-HTTP services can now be managed through the same Gateway API model as HTTP and gRPC traffic. ([cilium/cilium#46184](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46184), [cilium/cilium#46970](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46970), @eminaktas; [cilium/cilium#47381](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/47381), @arybolovlev)\r\n    * 🔐 **External Authorization**: HTTPRoute requests can now be authenticated and authorized through an external service before they reach the application, using the Gateway API `ExternalAuth` filter from GEP-1494. ([cilium/cilium#45739](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45739), @gauteoh)\r\n    * 🎛️ **More HTTP Traffic Controls**: HTTPRoutes gain native CORS support and additional 303, 307 and 308 redirect codes. `CiliumGatewayClassConfig` can also overwrite, append or preserve the HTTP Server response header. ([cilium/cilium#45924](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45924), @arybolovlev; [cilium/cilium#47232](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/47232), @youngnick; [cilium/cilium#46764](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46764), @xtineskim)\r\n    * ↔️ **Configurable gRPC-Web Translation**: `CiliumGatewayClassConfig` can disable automatic gRPC-web translation for Gateways that need to pass the original traffic through unchanged. Translation remains enabled by default. ([cilium/cilium#45984](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45984), @thorn3r)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🚠 **Networking**\r\n    * 🔌 **Extensible Datapath**: Datapath plugins let cloud providers extend or instrument Cilium's eBPF datapath with independently versioned programs, without maintaining a Cilium fork. ([cilium/cilium#45028](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45028), [cilium/cilium#45429](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45429), [cilium/cilium#45558](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45558), [cilium/cilium#46673](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46673), [cilium/cilium#46872](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46872), @jrife)\r\n    * 🚄 **Automatic Netkit Selection**: Set `bpf.datapathMode=auto` to use netkit automatically on supported kernels and fall back to veth on other nodes. The default remains veth. ([cilium/cilium#43062](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43062), @ajmmm)\r\n    * 📤 **Egress Gateway Improvements**: Dual-stack clusters can configure an explicit IPv6 egress IP. IPv4 Egress Gateway traffic also strictly honors the interface selected by the policy, matching the existing IPv6 behavior. ([cilium/cilium#44524](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44524), @yykkibbb; [cilium/cilium#45833](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45833), @julianwiedmann)\r\n    * 🔍 **Better BGP Tooling and Reconciliation**: New Hive shell commands expose route policies and clearer peer output formats. The control plane moves to GoBGP v4.6.1 and optimizes route-policy reconciliation. ([cilium/cilium#45628](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45628), [cilium/cilium#45534](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45534), @martonra; [cilium/cilium#45952](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45952), [cilium/cilium#46928](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46928), @rastislavs)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🌐 **IPAM and IP Selection**\r\n    * ☁️ **IPv6 for AWS ENI IPAM (Beta)**: Cilium can now allocate IPv6 prefixes to pods through AWS ENI IPAM, building on the move to the multi-pool allocator. ([cilium/cilium#46756](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46756), [cilium/cilium#46619](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46619), @41ks; [cilium/cilium#45154](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45154), [cilium/cilium#45958](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45958), @HadrienPatte)\r\n    * 🔄 **Migrate to Multi-Pool IPAM**: Existing clusters can move from cluster-pool IPAM to multi-pool IPAM without being rebuilt. ([cilium/cilium#46104](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46104), @pippolo84)\r\n    * 🧭 **Dynamic NodePort Source Addresses**: NodePort can use the kernel FIB to select the preferred source address dynamically before SNATing north-south traffic, on supported kernels. ([cilium/cilium#44625](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44625), @ldelossa)\r\n    * 🔵 **Prefer IPv6 Consistently**: The new top-level `preferIpv6` option applies to both Cilium health probes and Hubble peer communication when IPv4 and IPv6 are available. ([cilium/cilium#45447](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45447), @tibrezus)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🕸️ **Services and Cluster Mesh**\r\n    * 📍 **Topology-Aware Service Traffic**: Cilium's service load balancer now supports the Kubernetes `PreferSameZone` and `PreferSameNode` traffic distribution hints. ([cilium/cilium#44771](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44771), @raphink)\r\n    * ⚖️ **Weighted Maglev Backends**: Maglev now honors the `service.cilium.io/weight` annotation on EndpointSlices. A weight of zero drains new connections while allowing existing connections to continue. ([cilium/cilium#46061](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46061), @mhofstetter)\r\n    * 🌉 **More Load-Balancing Options with VXLAN**: Clusters using VXLAN can use per-Service load-balancing mode annotations, including compatible DSR configurations such as IPIP dispatch. ([cilium/cilium#45600](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45600), @brb)\r\n    * 🤖 **Stable MCS API Support**: Cilium's implementation of the Kubernetes Multi-Cluster Services API is now stable and recommended for portable service discovery through ClusterMesh. ([cilium/cilium#45404](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45404), [cilium/cilium#46953](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46953), @MrFreezeex)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🔒 **Encryption and Network Policy**\r\n    * 🚇 **Improved Ztunnel Identity Management**: The ztunnel path now supports either an internal certificate authority or SPIRE-issued workload identities, alongside new Prometheus metrics for enrollment and connection health. It transparently encrypts enrolled pod-to-pod connections, including traffic between pods on the same node. ([cilium/cilium#43763](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43763), [cilium/cilium#44275](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44275), [cilium/cilium#44136](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44136), [cilium/cilium#45861](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45861), @nddq; [cilium/cilium#45096](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45096), @gokulMSFT)\r\n    * 🛡️ **Kubernetes ClusterNetworkPolicy (KCNP)**: Cilium now supports the upstream, cluster-scoped policy API, including Admin and Baseline policy tiers alongside namespaced Kubernetes NetworkPolicy. ([cilium/cilium#42338](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42338), @TheBeeZee; [cilium/cilium#46253](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46253), @aanm)\r\n    * ⛔ **Friendlier IPv6 Policy Denials**: Cilium can return ICMPv6 Destination Unreachable responses when network policy denies IPv6 egress traffic, matching the IPv4 capability introduced in Cilium 1.19. ([cilium/cilium#44234](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44234), @Andreagit97)\r\n    * 🌐 **Select the Entire Cluster Mesh**: The new `cluster-mesh` policy entity selects every endpoint across a mesh, making cross-cluster security policies easier to express. ([cilium/cilium#46813](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46813), @squeed)\r\n    * ☁️ **Standardize AWS VPC Group Policies**: Policies that reference AWS VPC groups are now transformed into `CiliumCIDRGroup` resources, bringing external IP groups into the standard Cilium policy model. ([cilium/cilium#44704](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44704), @squeed)\r\n    * 🛂 **Per-Pod Source IP Verification Control**: Trusted workloads that need to send traffic with non-local source addresses can disable source IP verification through a pod annotation, guarded by a namespace-level opt-in. ([cilium/cilium#43505](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43505), @zbb88888)\r\n\r\n\r\n* ⚙️ **Day 2 Operations and Observability**\r\n    * 🔧 **Detect Configuration Drift**: Cilium can expose how many ConfigMap settings an agent has not yet applied, making it easier to identify when a restart is required after a configuration change. ([cilium/cilium#44703](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44703), @PhilipSchmid)\r\n    * ⏱️ **Understand Agent Start-Up Time**: New Hive lifecycle and Kubernetes resource-sync metrics show how long individual resources and components take to initialize. ([cilium/cilium#44802](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44802), [cilium/cilium#44639](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44639), @ILL1A)\r\n    * 🛰️ **Correlate More Hubble Verdicts with Policies**: Hubble can now identify the policy responsible for audit verdicts. ([cilium/cilium#45373](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45373), @Rajneesh180)\r\n    * 📊 **Standalone DNS Proxy Metrics**: The Standalone DNS Proxy now exports DNS metrics back through Cilium, improving visibility when DNS policy processing runs outside the agent. ([cilium/cilium#45222](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45222), [cilium/cilium#44601](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44601), @vipul-21)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🌅 **Performance and Scale**\r\n    * 💾 **Aggregated Load-Balancer State**: Cilium's internal backend representation has been flattened to efficiently support thousands of Services that share the same backends. ([cilium/cilium#44511](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/44511), @joamaki)\r\n    * 📡 **More Efficient Envoy Updates**: New ADS and Delta xDS modes provide consistent or incremental delivery of Envoy resources, reducing CPU usage and policy update latency. ([cilium/cilium#43887](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43887), @nezdolik; [cilium/cilium#45288](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45288), @jrajahalme)\r\n    * 🗂️ **Optimized BPF Policy Map Encoding**: Semantic wildcard identities for remote-node and world reduce BPF policy-map pressure, particularly in larger clusters. ([cilium/cilium#46438](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46438), @squeed)\r\n    * 📦 **Smaller CNI Binary**: The `cilium-cni` binary has been reduced from roughly 77 MB to 16 MB, shrinking both the Cilium image and its footprint on every node. ([cilium/cilium#45845](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45845), @giorio94)\r\n    * 🛠️ **Updated Dependencies and Foundations**: Cilium 1.20 updates to Kubernetes v1.36, Envoy v1.37.x, Gateway API v1.6.1, GoBGP v4.6.1 and MCS API v0.5.2. Runtime and builder images now use Ubuntu 26.04, and the default CNI configuration version moves from 0.3.1 to 1.0.0. ([cilium/cilium#45499](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45499), [cilium/cilium#47215](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/47215), @HadrienPatte; [cilium/cilium#45851](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45851), @sayboras; [cilium/cilium#47274](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/47274), @arybolovlev; [cilium/cilium#45952](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/45952), @rastislavs; [cilium/cilium#47166](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/47166), @MrFreezeex; [cilium/cilium#46971](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/46971), @gandro)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🏠 **Community**\r\n    * ❤️ **Production Case Studies**: Many end-users have told their stories running Cilium in production. Read and watch more from: [Asana](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mxjiSCnrb3c), [Etraveli](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KWS-NaZd62M&list=PLDg_GiBbAx-mSQHa1y9Z9sjBYUmG2FBCZ&index=7), [Michelin](https://www.cncf.io/case-studies/michelin-2/), [OpenAI](https://isovalent.com/blog/post/openai-isovalent-networking-kubernetes-case-study/), [Suse](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bgCg1tS72ME&list=PLDg_GiBbAx-mSQHa1y9Z9sjBYUmG2FBCZ&index=8), [Telefónica's acens](https://blogs.cisco.com/sp/telefonicas-acens-and-cisco-driving-the-next-generation-of-cloud-native-services-with-isovalent), and [Zynga](https://www.cncf.io/case-studies/zynga/). If your company wants to submit their case studies let us know. (Backport PR cilium/cilium#47397, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#47204, @ajmmm)\r\n* Fix bug where BGP Migration may deadlock (Backport PR cilium/cilium#47374, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#46897, @weizhoublue)\r\n* Fix potential hangs caused by Netlink errors in filterAndDestroySockets. (Backport PR cilium/cilium#47179, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#46967, @ysksuzuki)\r\n* fix(policy): add cluster-mesh to the Entity enum validation (Backport PR cilium/cilium#47397, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#47225, @doniacld)\r\n* gateway-api: fix redirect guards affecting route ordering for similar HTTPRoute matches. redundant rp_filter assertion from privileged test (Backport PR cilium/cilium#47374, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#47106, @Avr8)\r\n* envoy: add ads locality test (Backport PR cilium/cilium#47179, renamed leader-election lease-lock retrieval error (Backport PR cilium/cilium#47397, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#47249, @aanm)\r\n\r\n**Misc Changes:**\r\n* .github: allow fork PR checkout with cilium/cilium#47179, Upstream PR cilium/cilium#46928, @rastislavs)\r\n* bugtool: collect snmp6 and dev_snmp6 for IPv6 host packet drops (Backport PR cilium/cilium#47397, Upstream PR of Changes\r\n------------------\r\n\r\n**Major Changes:**\r\n* (Beta) ENI IPAM mode with IPv6: The ENI IPAM mode now supports allocating and using IPv6 prefixes. (cilium/cilium#46756, @41ks)\r\n* As previously announced, docker libnetwork plugin as been sunset and is no longer available. (cilium/cilium#46489, @aanm)\r\n* envoy: Add ADS (Aggregated Discovery Service) server support (cilium/cilium#43887, @nezdolik)\r\n* gateway-api: Add TCPRoute and UDPRoute support (cilium/cilium#46184, @eminaktas)\r\n* GatewayAPI: Implement ListenerSets. Allows per-namespace control of many Listeners for a single Gateway (cilium/cilium#46303, @asauber)\r\n* Introduce datapath plugins to enable third party datapath extensions. (cilium/cilium#46673, @jrife)\r\n* Makes the `remote-node` and `world` policy entities much more efficient by adding a new type of wildcard entry in the policy map. This can reduce policy map usage by thousands of entries in certain circumstances. (cilium/cilium#46438, @squeed)\r\n\r\n**Minor Changes:**\r\n* Add --reply and --not-reply flags to hubble observe to filter flows by reply direction. (cilium/cilium#46531, @amaanx86)\r\n* Add `spec.allowFirstIP` and `spec.allowLastIP` to `CiliumPodIPPool` to allow Multi-Pool IPAM users to make the first and last IPs of each allocated pod CIDR allocatable. (cilium/cilium#46323, @SivagurunathanV)\r\n* Add opt-in Helm value `nativeRoutingCIDRFromClusterPool` to derive the native routing CIDR from the cluster-pool IPAM CIDR when it is not explicitly configured. (cilium/cilium#46419, @Harsh63870)\r\n* Add support to migrate a cluster from cluster-pool IPAM mode to multi-pool IPAM mode. (cilium/cilium#46104, @pippolo84)\r\n* Added some commands to cilium-dbg to help diagnose policy map pressure. (cilium/cilium#46038, @squeed)\r\n* Added the `policy/mapstate/stage` command to `cilium-dbg script`. It can show the difference in policy map entries that would be created by a proposed set of policies. (cilium/cilium#46762, @squeed)\r\n* Allow CIDR selectors in network policies to match in-cluster IPs (cilium/cilium#45194, @TheBeeZee)\r\n* azure: Return actual public IP from AssignPublicIPAddresses{VMSS,VM} (cilium/cilium#46327, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* bgp: migrate CRDs storage version from v2alpha1 to v2 (cilium/cilium#46763, @martonra)\r\n* cilium-dbg: add table-json format to bgp/peers and bgp/routes (cilium/cilium#45858, @martonra)\r\n* clustermesh: update MCS-API to v0.5.1 (cilium/cilium#46533, @MrFreezeex)\r\n* daemon: allow --local-router-ipv4/6 without --enable-endpoint-routes (cilium/cilium#46082, @zbb88888)\r\n* datapath/l2responder: use l3 sockets for solicited node multicast signaling (cilium/cilium#46332, @bersoare)\r\n* Different named port numbers for a name from multiple pods are now honored in policy enforcement. (cilium/cilium#45760, @jrajahalme)\r\n* Endpoints created by the CNI plugin are no longer addressable by legacy container-id or pod-name identifiers via the Cilium agent API. Use `cni-attachment-id:<containerID>:<ifName>` or `cep-name:<namespace/name>` instead. (cilium/cilium#46810, @tklauser)\r\n* Extend the information reported by the troubleshoot kvstore and clustermesh commands (cilium/cilium#46516, @giorio94)\r\n* Fix operator not exporting k8s_client metrics (api latency, rate limiter latency, api calls total). (cilium/cilium#46353, @hanapedia)\r\n* gateway-api: add support for configuring Gateway access logs through the new `spec.telemetry.accessLogs` field in CiliumGatewayClassConfig. (cilium/cilium#46403, @arybolovlev)\r\n* Gateway-API: Added useRemoteAddress support. (cilium/cilium#45816, @s3rius)\r\n* gateway-api: Gateway status now reports unsupported listener protocols more precisely. (cilium/cilium#46815, @arybolovlev)\r\n* gateway-api: improve route/gateway listener protocol mismatch message (cilium/cilium#46697, @arybolovlev)\r\n* gateway-api: reject unsupported Gateway infrastructure.parametersRef (cilium/cilium#46730, @arybolovlev)\r\n* metrics: Emit native histograms for all histograms by default (cilium/cilium#46814, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* Miscellaneous improvements to the Cluster Mesh TLS certificates helm settings (cilium/cilium#46464, @giorio94)\r\n* Support configuring Envoy cluster default max pending requests via `envoy.clusterMaxPendingRequests` helm value or cilium-agent `--proxy-cluster-max-connections` command-line arg (cilium/cilium#45625, @0xch4z)\r\n* Support strict ingress encryption in native routing mode (cilium/cilium#46415, @rgo3)\r\n* The Cilium agent now waits for a static IP to be assigned before becoming ready if one is requested. (cilium/cilium#46636, @41ks)\r\n* The local REST BGP APIs are deprecated. It will be removed in the future. of Changes\r\n------------------\r\n\r\n**Major Changes:**\r\n* Gateway API: ExternalAuth filter support for HTTPRoutes  Cilium now supports the `ExternalAuth` filter type in HTTPRoutes (GEP-1494). Routes can delegate authorization decisions to an external service using either gRPC (Envoy ext_authz protocol) or HTTP. Configurable options include allowed request/response headers, request body forwarding, path prefix, and TLS-backed auth backends via `BackendTLSPolicy`. (cilium/cilium#45739, @gauteoh)\r\n* proxy: Bump cilium-envoy to v1.37.x (cilium/cilium#45851, @sayboras)\r\n\r\n**Minor Changes:**\r\n* Add `encryption.ztunnel.ca.type` Helm value to select ztunnel's CA backend (`spire`|`internal`, default `internal`). (cilium/cilium#45861, @nddq)\r\n* Add `helm.sh/chart` standard label (cilium/cilium#45771, @chernetskyi)\r\n* Adds optional fields for subject and private key options in certificate template when cert-manager is used to generate clustermesh-apiserver and hubble TLS certificates. (cilium/cilium#45972, @owayss)\r\n* alibabacloud: Migrate IP/CIDR fields to ip.Addr/Prefix wrappers (cilium/cilium#46210, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* Azure IPAM: track subnet once per AzureInterface on CiliumNode status, matching AWS and AlibabaCloud IPAM models. The previous per-address `addresses[].subnet` and flat `interfaces[].cidr` fields are deprecated. (cilium/cilium#45985, @jaredledvina)\r\n* azure: Remove duplicate `GetInstance` call in per-instance resync (cilium/cilium#46192, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* azure: Skip listing NIC of empty VMSSs (cilium/cilium#46129, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* bgp: Bump GoBGP from v3 to v4.5.0 (cilium/cilium#45952, @rastislavs)\r\n* clustermesh/docs: Improve Cluster Mesh intro documentation (cilium/cilium#46021, @MrFreezeex)\r\n* Extend default APIInteractions metric buckets from 10s to 2min (cilium/cilium#46115, @kamilWyszynski1)\r\n* feat(sdp): Support DNS metrics from Standalone DNS Proxy (cilium/cilium#44601, @vipul-21)\r\n* fqdn: Deprecate toFQDNs pre-cache flag and remove preflight  poller (cilium/cilium#45295, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* gateway-api: Add HTTPRoute CORS filter support. (cilium/cilium#45924, @arybolovlev)\r\n* gateway-api: add support for disabling gRPC-web translation (cilium/cilium#45984, @thorn3r)\r\n* helm: Remove loadBalancer.standalone option (cilium/cilium#46070, @joestringer)\r\n* ipam: Add CIDR-based release mechanism for ENI multipool mode (cilium/cilium#45958, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* ipam: Switch ENI IPAM from CRD to multi-pool allocator (cilium/cilium#45154, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* lb: support EndpointSlice weights for Maglev backends (cilium/cilium#46061, @mhofstetter)\r\n* policies that reference AWS VPC groups are now transformed in to a CiliumCIDRGroup. (cilium/cilium#44704, @squeed)\r\n* Prevent premature LRU eviction of newly inserted socket reverse NAT entries by touching the entry after insertion to set the LRU reference bit. (cilium/cilium#46228, @luoxuanqiang)\r\n* Relax DisableCiliumEndpointCRD to work with CES and operator slim mode (cilium/cilium#45698, @kamilWyszynski1)\r\n* Replace boringcrypto with upstream Go crypto libraries (cilium/cilium#46092, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* Shrink cilium-cni binary size by 80% (cilium/cilium#45845, @giorio94)\r\n* Updates the CiliumPodIPPool CRD version to v2. Adds a new per-CIDR configuration option \"reservedRanges\". (cilium/cilium#44383, @kyounghoonJang)\r\n* When IPv4 traffic exits an Egress Gateway node, it strictly uses the network interface specified in the CiliumEgressGatewayPolicy (or the default interface). This matches the behavior for IPv6 traffic. (cilium/cilium#45833, @julianwiedmann)\r\n* wireguard:mtu: fix mtu calculation with potential padding (cilium/cilium#45940, @smagnani96)\r\n\r\n**Bugfixes:**\r\n* Always add cluster label to node when `nodeSelectorLabels` is enabled to fix CiliumNetworkPolicy with `fromNodes`/`toNodes` with  `policy-default-local-cluster` enabled (enabled by default in 1.19+) (cilium/cilium#46068, @MrFreezeex)\r\n* azure: Fix public IP reassignment failure loop on operator restart (cilium/cilium#46240, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* bgp: Don't provide default_gateway reconciler when disabled (cilium/cilium#45911, @YutaroHayakawa)\r\n* bgp: Reduce amount of soft peer resets by service reconciliation and fix potentially missed incorrect metadata update upon failed reconciliation. (cilium/cilium#45927, @rastislavs)\r\n* bpf: don't silently drop packets with tcx hooks (cilium/cilium#45740, @Andreagit97)\r\n* bpf: egressgw: don't use bpf_redirect_neigh() for L3 packets (cilium/cilium#45703, @julianwiedmann)\r\n* bpf: fix host proxy packet routing to pods (cilium/cilium#45916, @atykhyy)\r\n* bug: fixed weighted backend traffic splitting for TLSRoute passthrough listeners in Gateway API (cilium/cilium#45937, @nickolaev)\r\n* cilium-dbg: `cilium map list` now displays \"unknown\" instead of 0 for maps that do not support cache-based entry counting. (cilium/cilium#44951, @skymensch)\r\n* datapath/mtu: add altname to mark cilium owned interfaces and do skip changing MTU on interfaces not managed by cilium (cilium/cilium#45799, @bersoare)\r\n* Fix a bug that causes the NamespaceSelector field in a CiliumEgressGatewayPolicy to be corrupted, and no longer effective. (cilium/cilium#45926, @julianwiedmann)\r\n* Fix a rare bug in clustermesh-apiserver that triggers incorrect deletion of a valid endpoint entry from the etcd under high pod churn (cilium/cilium#45780, @adamwathieu)\r\n* Fix allowedRoute namespace and kind restrictions on multi-listener Gateways. (cilium/cilium#45693, @eufriction)\r\n* Fix BGP PeerConfig status cleanup so it no longer times out when there are no managed conditions to remove. (cilium/cilium#45967, @ysksuzuki)\r\n* Fix BPF compilation failure on transient direct routing device address loss (cilium/cilium#44894, @christarazi)\r\n* Fix BPF LB map key collision where HostPort/NodePort expansion could overwrite a LoadBalancer frontend when the node IP matches the LoadBalancer external IP (e.g. k3s/RKE2 L2 ServiceLB). Also fix a ~30-minute NodePort outage that occurred after deleting a LoadBalancer whose external IP was a node address with a port in the NodePort range. (cilium/cilium#45314, @syedazeez337)\r\n* Fix bug that would disrupt node connectivity when ClusterIP/LoadBalancer VIPs overlapped with node-local IP addresses. (cilium/cilium#45572, @ajmmm)\r\n* Fix Cilium node IPv6 selection silently picking an address that failed duplicate-address detection, which could result in the node advertising an address belonging to another node (cilium/cilium#45868, @ssam18)\r\n* Fix dedicated Ingress reconciliation panic on invalid TLS passthrough rules (cilium/cilium#45737, @weizhoublue)\r\n* Fix Hubble metrics `labelsContext` parsing: values must now be comma-separated (e.g. `labelsContext=source_ip,destination_ip`). Previously, mixing `,` and `|` in the value would silently produce invalid tokens. (cilium/cilium#45809, @bitflicker64)\r\n* Fix Kubernetes ClusterNetworkPolicy (network-policy-api, alpha) match expressions (matchExpressions) being ignored when selecting endpoints. An \"In\" match selected no endpoints (e.g. a Deny rule would not block its intended traffic) and a \"NotIn\" match selected all endpoints, so policies using match expressions were not enforced as written. CiliumNetworkPolicy, CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy, and standard Kubernetes NetworkPolicy are not affected. (cilium/cilium#46253, @aanm)\r\n* fix(gateway-api): set ready condition in endpointSlice to true (cilium/cilium#46237, @ulrichgiraud)\r\n* fix: nil pointer dereference panic due to uninitialized logger (cilium/cilium#45782, @weizhoublue)\r\n* Fixed unsolicited IPv6 L2 announcements ignored by receiving hosts, as not conformant to RFC 4861 (cilium/cilium#46079, @giorio94)\r\n* Fixes a bug where policymap pressure was incorrectly being reported as 0. (cilium/cilium#45791, @squeed)\r\n* gateway-api: fix GatewayClass field index (cilium/cilium#46127, @thorn3r)\r\n* gateway-api: Fix silent drops of routes on multi listener gateways (cilium/cilium#45821, @weizhoublue)\r\n* iptables: match wireguard packets by proto+port instead of packet mark (cilium/cilium#45974, @bersoare)\r\n* multipool: Fix retries for CiliumNode Get errors (cilium/cilium#46124, @pippolo84)\r\n* operator/ipam: Avoid short-lived ctx for allocators start (cilium/cilium#46034, @pippolo84)\r\n* Revert Gateway-API/Ingress endpointslice removal (incl. restore This requires an annotation on the pod's Namespace as well. (cilium/cilium#43505, @zbb88888)\r\n* gateway-api: Cilium now supports Gateway API v1.5.1. Please note that this includes moving TLSRoute to v1. To continue safely using TLSRoute, you _must_ migrate from TLSRoute v1alpha2 to TLSRoute v1. (cilium/cilium#45251, @youngnick)\r\n\r\n**Minor Changes:**\r\n* Add generic `preferIpv6` Helm value / `--prefer-ipv6` agent flag to prefer IPv6 addresses for health probes and Hubble peer communication when both address families are available. The `hubble.preferIpv6` Helm value and `--hubble-prefer-ipv6` agent flag are deprecated in favor of the new top-level `preferIpv6` and will be removed in Cilium 1.20. (cilium/cilium#45447, @tibrezus)\r\n* agent, devices: make children interfaces bound to VRF interfaces eligible for device auto-selection. (cilium/cilium#45146, @ldelossa)\r\n* bgp: add --format option to the bgp/peers command to support table, json, and detailed output formats (cilium/cilium#45534, @martonra)\r\n* bgp: Add bgp/route-policies shell command (cilium/cilium#45628, @martonra)\r\n* bpf: add HostPort wildcard lookup to per-packet LB (cilium/cilium#45115, @saiaunghlyanhtet)\r\n* bpf: Move edt_set_aggregate() above proto switch in cil_from_container (cilium/cilium#45129, @brb)\r\n* ces: remove deprecated `ces-slice-mode` option (cilium/cilium#45512, @smagnani96)\r\n* chore(deps): update cilium-envoy dependency (cilium/cilium#45116, @sayboras)\r\n* cilium-agent: when `--k8s-service-proxy-name` is set, `EndpointSlices` are now filtered by the `service.kubernetes.io/service-proxy-name` label at the watch level, matching how `Services` are already filtered, operators with hand-managed `EndpointSlices` must stamp the matching label on those slices. (cilium/cilium#45504, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* clustermesh: Cilium MCS-API implementation now use the v1beta1 CRDs instead of v1alpha1 (cilium/cilium#45404, @MrFreezeex)\r\n* daemon: remove deprecated `k8s-api-server` option (cilium/cilium#45511, @smagnani96)\r\n* egressgateway: Add IPv6 support for specifying egress IP in CiliumEgressGatewayPolicy (cilium/cilium#44524, @yykkibbb)\r\n* encryption: remove deprecated `enable-encryption-strict-mode` `encryption-strict-mode-cidr` and `encryption-strict-mode-allow-remote-node-identities` options (cilium/cilium#45452, @smagnani96)\r\n* feat(sdp): Add metrics for standalone dns proxy (cilium/cilium#45222, @vipul-21)\r\n* Fix transaction handling and path usage in loadbalancer script tests. (cilium/cilium#45230, @08volt)\r\n* helm: allow configuring cilium-envoy probe timeouts (cilium/cilium#44231, @madmecodes)\r\n* helm: install ValidatingAdmissionPolicy when GatewayAPI is enabled (cilium/cilium#45446, @thorn3r)\r\n* hubble: correlate policy for audit verdicts and implicit denies (cilium/cilium#45373, @Rajneesh180)\r\n* ipsec: remove deprecated `enable-ipsec-encrypted-overlay` option (cilium/cilium#45449, @smagnani96)\r\n* iptables-based masquerading: Ensure iptables rules respect longest prefix match by sorting routes by mask length when enable-masquerade-to-route-source is enabled (cilium/cilium#45192, @liyihuang)\r\n* k8s: Validate empty (C)CNPs at admission (cilium/cilium#45145, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* kpr: Allow users to use VXLAN in LBModeAnnotation mode (cilium/cilium#45600, @brb)\r\n* lb: remove deprecated `node-port-algorithm` and `node-port-mode` (cilium/cilium#45509, @smagnani96)\r\n* metrics: Add high-level hive metrics (cilium/cilium#44802, @ILL1A)\r\n* metrics: add instance_name and local_asn label to BGP Control Plane metrics and delete the vrouter label (cilium/cilium#45268, @martonra)\r\n* metrics: Add metric for k8s resource sync duration (cilium/cilium#44639, @ILL1A)\r\n* metrics: fix operator DumpMetrics to report histogram/summary quantiles (cilium/cilium#45100, @skymensch)\r\n* operator/spire: make SPIRE client configurable for ztunnel (cilium/cilium#44136, @nddq)\r\n* pkg/endpoint: skip logger rebuild on policy revision updates (cilium/cilium#45533, @sjohnsonpal)\r\n* Remove deprecated `v2alpha1` `CiliumNodeConfig` API that was promoted to `v2` in cilium 1.16. (cilium/cilium#45215, @08volt)\r\n* Fix cilium-agent crash when a transient network error occurs during CiliumNode update. The agent now retries instead of calling Fatal. **Release Announcement** 🎉: We are excited to announce the [Cilium 1.19.0](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/releases/tag/v1.19.0) release!\r\n\r\nA total of **2934 new commits** have been contributed to this release by a growing community of over **1010 developers** and over **23,600 GitHub stars**! 🤩\r\n\r\n⚠️ You may need to take action during upgrade to Cilium v1.19 if you use Network Policies, Cluster Mesh, LoadBalancer IPAM or BGP. See the [Upgrade Guide](https://docs.cilium.io/en/v1.19/operations/upgrade/#upgrade-notes) for more details.\r\n\r\nThe full changelog can be found [here](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/blob/v1.19/CHANGELOG.md).\r\n\r\nHere are some of the highlights:\r\n\r\n* 🛡️ **Network Policy**\r\n    * 🃏 **Multi-Level DNS Matches**: DNS Policies match pattern now support a wildcard prefix(*`**.`*) to match multilevel subdomain as pattern prefix. ([cilium/cilium#43420](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43420), @fristonio)\r\n    * 📡 **Match New Protocols**: You can now match VRRP and IGMP protocols in host firewall rules. ([cilium/cilium#39872](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/39872), @aditighag; [cilium/cilium#41949](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/41949), @kyounghunJang)\r\n    * ⛔ **Actively Deny Connections**: When Network Policies deny a connection, Cilium can return ICMPv4 \"Destination unreachable\" messages for a friendlier deny. ([cilium/cilium#41406](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/41406), @antonipp)\r\n    * 🌐 **Select Clusters Explicitly**: When network policy selectors don't explicitly define a cluster for communication to be allowed, they will now default to only allowing the local cluster. ([cilium/cilium#40609](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/40609), @MrFreezeex)\r\n    * 🔧 **Unlock Future Work**: This release brings several internal improvements to the network policy engine in preparation for features planned in the next Cilium minor release ([cilium/cilium#39906](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/39906), @vipul-21; [cilium/cilium#42784](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42784), [cilium/cilium#42896](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42896), @jrajahalme)\r\n    * ⚠️ **Deprecate underutilized features**: To focus on solving common problems Cilium users face, this release deprecates the Kafka protocol match fields (beta), as well as the `ToRequires` and `FromRequires` policy fields. ([cilium/cilium#43167](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43167), @sayboras; [cilium/cilium#40967](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/40967), @TheBeeZee)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🔒 **Encryption & Authentication**\r\n    * 🔐 **Encryption Strict Modes**: Both IPsec and WireGuard transparent encryption modes now support a \"strict mode\" to require traffic to be encrypted between nodes. Unencrypted traffic will be dropped in this mode. ([cilium/cilium#39239](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/39239), [cilium/cilium#42115](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42115), @rgo3, @julianwiedmann)\r\n    * 🚇 **Ztunnel Beta**: You can enroll namespaces into Ztunnel, which enables TCP connections between workloads to be transparently encrypted and authenticated. ([cilium/cilium#42766](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42766), [cilium/cilium#42819](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42819), [cilium/cilium#43227](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43227) and others,  @ldelossa, @rgo3, @nddq)\r\n    * 👥 **Mutual Authentication**: The out-of-band [Mutual Authentication](https://docs.cilium.io/en/v1.19/network/servicemesh/mutual-authentication/mutual-authentication/) feature is now disabled by default, pending community feedback. If you have a requirement for mTLS, consider trying the new Ztunnel integration. ([cilium/cilium#42665](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42665), @christarazi)\r\n    * ↪️ **Accelerate IPsec**: The IPsec encryption mode now supports BPF Host Routing for faster route lookups ([cilium/cilium#41997](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/41997), @pchaigno)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🚠 **Networking**\r\n    * 🚀  **BIG TCP in Tunnels**: Leverage upcoming Linux support for BIG TCP when communicating over UDP-based tunnels such as VXLAN and Geneve. ([cilium/cilium#43416](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43416), @gentoo-root)\r\n    * 🥌 **Packetization-Layer Path MTU Discovery**: Detect maximum transmission unit (MTU) sizes for network paths using TCP. ([cilium/cilium#42012](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42012), [cilium/cilium#43710](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43710), @tommyp1ckles)\r\n    * 🚆 **IPv6 Underlay**: You can now choose IPv6 for the tunnel underlay address family on dual-stack clusters. ([cilium/cilium#40324](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/40324), @pchaigno)\r\n    * 🏷️ **Multi-Pool IPAM is ready for wider use**: Update the Multi-Pool IPAM feature to work with IPsec and direct routing modes, and promote it from Beta to Stable. ([cilium/cilium#40460](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/40460), [cilium/cilium#42191](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42191), @pippolo84)\r\n    * 🎭 **More Configurable Masquerade**: IP Masquerade configuration can now be customized for traffic sent to nodes in other IP subnets, and addresses in IPAM pools can be excluded from masquerade ([cilium/cilium#37568](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/37568), @behzad-mir; [cilium/cilium#43380](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43380), @alimehrabikoshki)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🕸️ **Services and Service Mesh**\r\n    * 📣 **Layer-2 Announcements**: Add support for Neighbor Discovery Advertisements for IPv6 Layer-2 Announcements. ([cilium/cilium#39648](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/39648), @msune)\r\n    * 🔁 **IPv6 Service Loopback**: Pods can now connect to themselves via a Kubernetes \"loopback service\" using IPv6. ([cilium/cilium#39594](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/39594), @saiaunghlyanhtet)\r\n    * ⛩️ **Gateway API Enhancements**: Cilium's GAMMA support now includes support for using GRPCRoute as well as HTTPRoute. ([cilium/cilium#41936](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/41936), @youngnick)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🛣️ **Border Gateway Protocol (BGP)**\r\n    * 🔌 **Advertise Addresses from Interfaces**: There's a new Interface BGP advertisement type that allows advertisement of IPs assigned on local interfaces. This can be useful for example in multi-homing setups, where a common node's loopback address can be advertised via multiple BGP sessions over different network interfaces. ([cilium/cilium#42469](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42469), @rastislavs)\r\n    * ✉️ **Override Source IP addresses**: You can override the auto-generated BGP session source IP with the IP address applied on the configured `sourceInterface` to allow binding the BGP connection to the loopback address which is not tied to the specific physical interface's lifecycle ([cilium/cilium#42583](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42583), @rastislavs)\r\n    * 🔁 **Withdraw Empty Routes**: Optionally withdraw BGP routes when a service has 0 endpoints, to allow balancing to a different DC/cluster with `externalTrafficPolicy=Cluster` ([cilium/cilium#40717](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/40717), @oblazek)\r\n    * ⚠️ **Move to `cilium.io/v2` API**: The support for the older `CiliumBGPPeeringPolicy` v1 API is now removed and should be replaced with v2 APIs. ([cilium/cilium#42278](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42278), @rastislavs)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🛰️ **Observability**\r\n    * 🔬 **Trace IP Options**: Configure Cilium and Hubble to trace specific packets through the cluster using IP Options. ([cilium/cilium#41306](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/41306), @Bigdelle)\r\n    * 🚩 **Filter Encrypted Flows**: Filter flows when using the `hubble` command line to understand the encryption status of the traffic, either `--encrypted` or `--unencrypted`. ([cilium/cilium#43096](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43096), @SRodi)\r\n    * 🔖 **Tag Drops with Policy Names**: Hubble v1.Events drop messages now include which Network Policy caused the drop. ([cilium/cilium#41693](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/41693), @41ks)\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🌅 **Performance and Scale**\r\n    * ⚡ **Faster Network Policy Computation**: Improve Cilium resource usage for handling selectors in network policies. ([cilium/cilium#42008](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42008), @jrajahalme; [cilium/cilium#42580](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42580), @odinuge)\r\n    * 🔌 **More Efficient Connection Tracking**: Several improvements have been made to reduce the number of connections being tracked by Cilium, particularly when using Geneve, VXLAN or WireGuard. ([cilium/cilium#38782](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/38782), @BenoitKnecht; [cilium/cilium#41990](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/41990), @bersoare)\r\n    * 💾 **Better Scale in AWS**: Reduce memory usage for cilium-operator in large AWS environments with many resources. ([cilium/cilium#42529](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42529), @liyihuang)\r\n\r\n\r\n* ⚙️ **Operations**\r\n    * 📦 **Access Helm charts via Registry**: Helm charts are also available under `quay.io/cilium/charts/cilium` ([cilium/cilium#43624](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43624), @aanm)\r\n    * 📊 **Metrics Encryption**: Add TLS/mTLS support for Prometheus metrics exposed by the Cilium Operator. ([cilium/cilium#42077](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42077), @phuhung273)\r\n    * 🤖 **Easier Multi-Cluster install**: There's now support for auto-installing the Custom Resource Definitions (CRDs) for Multi-Cluster  Services (MCS). ([cilium/cilium#40729](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/40729), @MrFreezeex)\r\n    * 📜 **Simpler Certificate Management**: Streamline Cluster Mesh and Hubble certificate generation when using GitOps approaches. ([cilium/cilium#42298](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42298), @MrFreezeex)\r\n    * 🛠️ **Cilium dependencies** were updated to Kubernetes v1.35, Envoy v1.35, Gateway API v1.4, and GoBGP v3.37. ([cilium/cilium#43422](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/43422), @aanm; [cilium/cilium#40569](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/40569), @sayboras; [cilium/cilium#41936](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/41936), @youngnick; [cilium/cilium#42824](https://github.com/cilium/cilium/pull/42824), @rastislavs).\r\n\r\n\r\n* 🏠 **Community**\r\n    * ❤️ **Production Case Studies**: Many end-users have stepped forward to tell their stories running Cilium in production. If your company wants to submit their case studies let us know. (cilium/cilium#42784, @jrajahalme)\r\n* Policy engine adds support for tiers and pass verdicts for ClusterNetworkPolicies (cilium/cilium#42896, @jrajahalme)\r\n* ztunnel: add ZDS server (cilium/cilium#42364, @rgo3)\r\n\r\n**Minor Changes:**\r\n* Add the new flags of eni and ipam for cilium agent, and users can use helm to configure the agent directly without using custom CNI (cilium/cilium#43086, @liyihuang)\r\n* bpf:hubble: support policy verdict from L3 devices (cilium/cilium#43015, @smagnani96)\r\n* ces: remove controller maximum limits on QPS (cilium/cilium#43154, @jshr-w)\r\n* Cilium Preflight check no longer includes Envoy Configmaps, making it easier to correctly run. (cilium/cilium#43153, @youngnick)\r\n* clustermesh:  prevent redirecting to endpoints from clusters not exposing certain ports on a port conflict in the MCS-API context (cilium/cilium#43251, @MrFreezeex)\r\n* clustermesh: add trafficDistribution and internalTrafficPolicy to MCS (cilium/cilium#43233, @MrFreezeex)\r\n* clustermesh: MCS now raise a port conflict when exported Service ports are not an exact match (cilium/cilium#43414, @MrFreezeex)\r\n* Correctly handle Gateways with a nil selector when they set NamespacesFromSelector (cilium/cilium#43085, @phuhung273)\r\n* DNS Policies match pattern now support a wildcard prefix(`**.`) to match multilevel subdomain as pattern prefix. For example: `**.cilium.io` now selects both `app.cilium.io`` and `test.app.cilium.io` (cilium/cilium#43420, @fristonio)\r\n* eni: mitigate a race-condition where secondary ENI interface did not get rp_filter disabled (cilium/cilium#43203, @marseel)\r\n* envoy: Allow customize per cluster connections / requests limit (cilium/cilium#43049, @exherb)\r\n* gateway-api: Add retry with exponential backoff for CRD discovery when API server is temporarily unreachable during operator startup (cilium/cilium#43452, @lexfrei)\r\n* hubble: Add --encrypted and --unencrypted flags to filter flows by encryption status (cilium/cilium#43096, @SRodi)\r\n* hubble: add VNI information for Geneve and VXLAN to Hubble flow (cilium/cilium#43398, @glrf)\r\n* k8s: add support for labels specific to cilium-secret namespaces (cilium/cilium#43106, @jusch23)\r\n* multi-pool: add support for excluding IP pools from masquerade (cilium/cilium#43380, @alimehrabikoshki)\r\n* operator: Improve AWS API pagination with adaptive fallback (cilium/cilium#42127, @pillai-ashwin)\r\n* operator: Refactor unmanaged kube-dns pods management into a separate cell. The `unmanaged-pod-watcher-interval` flag now expects a duration value. (cilium/cilium#43344, @HadrienPatte)\r\n* runtime: Add libatomic1 for cilium-envoy dependency (cilium/cilium#43292, @sayboras)\r\n* Some of the secrets generated by Helm use functions that are not idempotent. They are now labelled with `cilium.io/helm-template-non-idempotent: \"true\"` so that they can easily get identified. (cilium/cilium#43156, @fgiloux)\r\n* ztunnel: add ztunnel as an encryption enum for cilium status (cilium/cilium#43227, @nddq)\r\n\r\n**Bugfixes:**\r\n* bpf: Align LRU map sizing with kernel rounding rules (cilium/cilium#42511, @pmagne)\r\n* cgroup: don't start watch if KPRConfig.EnableSocketLB is disabled (cilium/cilium#43256, @mhofstetter)\r\n* CiliumEnvoyConfig proxy ports are now restored on agent restarts.
